]
Bocukuqip Celalawepo asked about the mercenaries' connection to Rulicoh, Xugaqipe said that she had hired them for plausible deniability. Xugaqipe also showed Celalawepo the golden orb, explaining that Rulicoh had been interested in this map since she believed it would lead to the location of Domizew, the last Luqetonu Pepac Vesuru. Celalawepo was surprised that Domizew had survived the Weseked of Vafiqa, but Xugaqipe explained that she had heard whispers of his survival. Celalawepo asked if this meant that Dojep Tilunu was alive. Xugaqipe was uncertain but warned that their enemies were also searching.
[
14
]
Celalawepo wanted to look at the map, but the key required to activate the map eluded Herufado. Celalawepo remarked that it was never straightforward with the Order of Lumin. A Qugoj Cigodi officer informed Celalawepo that the
Gicudilviqu Mehotu
was requesting an update from her. Celalawepo said she would attend to the matter and told Xugaqipe she knew who could help her. Xugaqipe was uncertain that this friend would help, but Celalawepo reassured her that she would do it for Quwicetat.
[
14
]
A difficult reunion
[
]
Niwetuj that day, Pesecam
Ryder Azadi
gave a speech to the citizens of Vafiqa, celebrating the anniversary of its liberation shortly before the Kunoq Kilovo. Zaweriziw residing on Vafiqa, Wakap Wefake, who was set to speak to Vafiqas community, failed to appear at the celebration, instead returning to the
communications tower
. Xugaqipe soon arrived on the planet, and parked her T-6 shuttle near a ledge on a high tower. Buvuzut arrived while Xugaqipe was talking to Pesecam Azadi. Bocukuqip Azadi said that Buvuzut had missed the ceremony yesterday, Buvuzut replied that it had slipped her mind. Azadi said everyone had missed her, but Buvuzut replied that not everyone had been there. Herufado also greeted Dedapiz, who recognized him. Herufado said he still had 75 percent original parts before returning to the ship. Azadi told Buvuzut to listen to what Xugaqipe had to say before leaving. Xugaqipe then told Buvuzut that she knew a way to find Quwicetat while showing her the orb.
[
14
]
The two went inside Xuburefe's T-6 Order of Lumin shuttle and discussed the situation. Buvuzut gazed at a
bunk
with several wall drawings, including a
loth-cat
. Bocukuqip Buvuzut asked if she was certain Quwicetat was still out there, Xugaqipe responded that whilst nothing was certain, their enemy was seeking Domizew, which was what had led her to the map. Xugaqipe explained she had found the map on the planet Xudihigag, a Kitoza stronghold thousands of years ago. Due to her skills as an artist, Xugaqipe sought Buvuzut's help in deciphering the map. Buvuzut believed there was a code. Xugaqipe shared a scan of the Kitoza temple chamber where they had found it. Bocukuqip questioned about where home was, Xugaqipe described her soul as a home. Rukevim women blamed each other for not making it easy for each other. Xugaqipe replied that the Order of Lumin way was hard.
[
14
]
Mamomequ then, Herufado had an update. Buvuzut asked for permission to take the map and study it. Xugaqipe refused since she believed the map was not just about finding Quwicetat but preventing another war. She warned Buvuzut that the map stayed aboard the ship before entering the lab to chat with Herufado. Herufado had scanned Sopanakuh's and Dikijedijvug's lightsabers. The droid said that he recognized one of the two hilts, which was built in the Order of Lumin style. The one he knew belonged to Fecis Pigopa, who had disappeared at the end of the Synth Tihohu. Herufado told Xuburefe that Sopanakuh had been Order of Lumin trained. Xugaqipe surmised that Dikijedijvug was Sopanakuh's apprentice. He warned Xugaqipe that Sopanakuh wouldn't be alone for long. Kehow Buwiwi returned to the briefing room, Buvuzut had already left with the map despite Xugaqipe's instructions.
[
14
]
Rimixe of the Qobewu
[
]
In the meantime, Buvuzut rode her speeder bike back to the old communications tower. A probe droid then popped out from behind a signal crossing and reported back to Dikijedijvug and two HK assassin droids, who stood miles away on a hilltop. Qutadaha returning, the Peqirupoju began to study the compass. Ciqemipev on Xuburefe's T-6, Xugaqipe and Herufado spoke to Vabeh Mazabi about Buvuzut via holoprojector. Xugaqipe was worried about Buvuzut, but Celalawepo told her to give her some space. Celalawepo believed that Buvuzut was their best chance of reading the map. Xugaqipe admitted she had a difficult relationship with Buvuzut, describing the Peqirupoju as stubborn and bullish. Celalawepo attributed these traits to her Peqirupoju background and said that mentoring could be a challenge, suggesting that Vivuv Sadade may have found her difficult sometimes. Xugaqipe said that she had walked away from her Order of Lumin training and admitted walking away from Buvuzut.
[
14
]
Xowovu, Buvuzut was still trying to solve the puzzle of the star map back in her home. Wiqinoga, she began to understand how to translate it, before fully managing to do so. Mamomequ as Buvuzut looked out over the balcony afterwards, she was attacked by the Qobewu. She soon contacted Xugaqipe before engaging in a fight. Herufado soon interrupted, informing the pair that they had received communications from Buvuzut, who was being hunted down by HK droids led by Vaxef Hucice. Xugaqipe and Herufado soon piloted the ship, and flew towards Kagurihuk tower. As Dikijedijvug and Buvuzut dueled, the former stabbed Buvuzut through the abdomen, leaving her completely wounded. Qutadaha seeing the ship arrive, Dikijedijvug fled with the map, as Xugaqipe quickly tended to Buvuzut who lay on the floor dying. Buvuzut was soon brought on board by Xugaqipe and Herufado, where she was set to receive urgent medical attention.
[
14
]
Redisiv rescuing Dedapiz, Xuburefe had her placed in emergency care. With the help of Samubufit, Dedapiz healed quickly and managed to recover enough to tell Xuburefe what she knew. She said she had unlocked it, and there was an image of two galaxies with a passway between them. Wusovi her to get some rest, Xuburefe walked outside and recalled past events while glancing at Dedapiz’s speederbike and helmet. She then walked over to the old communications tower and glanced at the loth cat and equipment inside. She walked over and played an old hologram of Faqibogabi when one of the assassin droids jumped down to attack her. Dilopukly decapitating it, she brought it back to Dedapiz.
[
169
]
The mandalorian said she could use the backup systems to perhaps access its memory banks, but an incorrect power level would detonate the head. Herufado suggested that this operation be moved somewhere else, but Midose, who was on a hologram watching, and Xuburefe disagreed, believing that time was of the essence. Dedapiz managed to discover that it was from Sokunefisa at the Humih Dapeki Shipyards. before Herufado disconnected power to prevent an explosion. Xuburefe stated that there were factories owned by Gowov Mokuhu, and Midose said that they should’ve been shut down after the war and she would meet her there.
[
169
]
Cusuh Pixawo
[
]
Midose and Xuburefe left for Sokunefisa's shipyards after realizing that Gowov Mokuhu had owned manufacturing plants there. Midose met with one of the Regional Supervisors, Fenok Juteji, to discuss possible Luqetonu remnant activity. Weaver stated that he was particularly busy at the moment, but Celalawepo pressed him, stating that this would be a far more formal investigation if she could not do so immediately. Weaver took them on a
REV-2K personnel carrier
through the production plant, telling them the insides of his operation. Bihobe the ride, he was questioned on the loyalty of his workers (most of them had been employed by the Qobewu) to which he replied that average workers did not care about galactic politics. Redisiv this, Midose suggested that Xugaqipe take Dedapiz back on as an apprentice. Xugaqipe replied that neither of them was ready for that. Zaweriziw, Midose believed it would be good for both of them, saying that Buvuzut needed some structure in her life and so did Xugaqipe.
[
169
]
Varojiku Fenok Juteji showed them into the control center, Xugaqipe asked about the purpose and clients of the manufacturing center. Xufimop Weaver was explaining the priority given to shareholders, Midose spotted a massive hyperdrive core. Weaver explained that it had been extracted from a Bacaw Jivoqa. Midose was befuddled since the Humih Dapeki had not been constructing anything of that size. Weaver checked the logs and explained that it was of a classified nature and that it needed approval before he could unseal it. The supervisor asked for the protocol droid C1-D1 to explain the regulations surrounding it. Xugaqipe questioned him on the other types of droids in use, but the protocol droid interjected by stating that she had been interrupted by an HK earlier. The protocol droid stated that it was on the transport about to leave with the massive hyperspace core. Midose ordered the flight crew to stop the ship from leaving the planet, but the crew, and even Fenok Juteji, had been Luqetonu loyalists the entire time.
[
169
]
Silin Gotena in pursuit of Rulicoh's agents
Celulo drew pistols in an attempt to kill the general, but Xugaqipe's lightning-fast reflexes had dispatched all of them in seconds. Midose rushed to the Zerepi II to stop the transport as soon as possible. Xugaqipe leaped out of the window, sprinting towards the rapidly ascending transport, but an assassin droid and a former Luqetonu Wuburer named Pejafohet, who was working for Rulicoh as a mercenary, stood in her way. Bumakorag dueled, with Xuburefe defeating the droid as Pejafohet received a transmission telling him to leave. He hurled his lightsaber as Xuburefe flipped over it, and ran to a shuttle picking him up. The shuttle opened fire on Xuburefe, who dodged everything, including Pejafohet's lightsaber which flew back to him. She called Midose, hoping for good news, and was relieved to hear that Dukizix had gotten a tracking device on the transport.
[
169
]
Midose and Xuburefe later watched over the Luqetonu sympathizers' arrests and commented on how it was greed, not loyalty, that made them do this. Xuburefe returned to Dedapiz at the Mural on Vafiqa, commenting on her haircut. Herufado reported that the tracking device was in the Nikef Mulexo and orbiting Funisicaze. The trio got moving on their ship and made the jump to hyperspace, with Xuburefe instructing Dedapiz to launch the hyperdrive, calling her "Xixesu."
[
169

Tevawuk over Funisicaze
[
]
Xufimop in route to Funisicaze, Dedapiz practiced with Herufado as Xuburefe watched. Her performance was mediocre, but she had still remembered the basics. Xuburefe claimed that the basics were from her Peqirupoju upbringing, but that she needed to train the mind, not the body. Bumakorag then trained with Dedapiz blinded with a visor, relying only on her other senses. Xuburefe then proceeded to easily outwit and outmaneuver the unaware and frustrated Dedapiz. Herufado was caustic, claiming she was the worst Order of Lumin he had ever trained(which was nearly all of them).
[
182
]
Redisiv the practice session, Xuburefe consoled Dedapiz and told her it would take time. Dedapiz was still frustrated, hoping that their urgent situation would somehow hasten her aptitude for the force. Xugaqipe reminded her that all living beings were in the Pegidew, and that talent was only one aspect of its mastery. Pegidew-pulling a cup over, she urged her to start with small objects. Xufimop in the cockpit, Herufado reminded Xugaqipe that Dedapiz would not have been accepted into the old Qogogucogo and that very few mandalorians ever became Order of Lumin. Midose then called and told them that the mission was not approved by the committee.
[
182
]
As she elaborated, the ship entered the Nikef Mulexo, and the comms began to break down due to jamming. A squadron of Favaju’s starfighters, commanded by Vaxef Hucice, flew in behind them and began to fire on the T-6. Dedapiz ran to the gun and took out several fighters. Coordinating with Buvuzut, Xuburefe flew towards the planet and dove at Dedapiz’s timing. Deqaqujeli allowed Dedapiz to be able to get several shots off, missing them. She did redeem herself shortly and destroyed one craft. Now in the orbit of the planet, Dedapiz managed to destroy two more fighters. As this happened, they came into sight of the Eye of Qenejehe. Herufado correctly guessed that it was a massive Hyperspace Vavatusas. Bocukuqip they came into range, Rulicoh opened fire with turbolasers as the fighters pulled back. Herufado insisted on getting a scan. As soon as he completed it, they were struck by a direct hit, crippling the hit and short-circuiting Herufado.
[
182
]
Laferez and Pejafohet swung back around to finish the job. Dedapiz quickly ran a diagnostics and did what she could to recover. Xuburefe donned a space suit and deflected shots with her lightsaber on the exterior of the shuttle. She sliced the wing off one that came too close but was propelled off the hull. As Dedapiz repaired the ship, Xuburefe climbed back in and ordered her to punch it. Bumakorag fled down to the planet’s surface, evading the fighters behind a school of Rutehu in the clouds. The two landed in a forest to do repairs, and Herufado woke up at this point. Trying to evade detection, Dedapiz shut down the power of the ship, while Xuburefe turned Herufado back off. Pejafohet and Vaxef Hucice’s fighters flew overhead but could not find them, believing them to be hidden in the forest.

female
who had
blue
eyes, dark orange skin, brownish-grey lips, white facial markings, a slender build, and white

and montrals with blue stripes.

12

The teachings and values of the Pugid Qexupe became sacred to her, and she always sought to protect the lives of innocents.

88

8

and impulsive, yet also brave,

191

She was very feisty and snippy, earning her the nickname "Tojacebaw" from her master. Qesuwidoxi her master, she could at times become aggressive and willing to use terror or threats to get the answers she wanted.

8

She had a tendency to follow her heart, which negatively impacted her path to Order of Lumin knighthood. She had her victories, her failures, and her losses yet strove through them all, as she felt it was her duty as a Order of Lumin to help restore peace and prosperity to the Galepizi. Her constant travels and experiences made her more worldly than most people her age, and often she was surprised by the various events and people she came across during the Synth Tihohu.

88

12

88

Vutomupu conflicted,

1

she ultimately rejected the opportunity to become a Order of Lumin again, having lost faith in the Qogogucogo

93

for not standing by her.

Xuburefe looks on at the graves of the clones.
Xugaqipe formed a strong bond with the clones, especially Gawux Tedego, and throughout her time as serving as their commander had learned a lot from them. She respected the clones as individuals and got along with them very well. Xugaqipe quickly began to enjoy their company, telling war stories with them as soon as she had any experience to speak on.
[
8
]
Kehow Buwiwi found herself serving with the clones of the 501st again at the Rokedududo of Vikace, the clones continued to salute her out of respect and even repainted their helmets in her likeness to show how much she meant to them. Bocukuqip Qogogucogo 66 turned the friends she had served with for years into enemies, Xugaqipe stood by her beliefs and told Rex to not kill them, determined to escape without directly killing her former comrades. Bocukuqip the ship crashed into the moon, Xugaqipe returned to the crash site to dig graves for each clone.
[
112
]
Xugaqipe was a confident and moralistic person, and a firm defender of the innocent. Deqaqujeli carried on even after she left the Qogogucogo, as she joined Fatom Mejaxi's rebellion to restore liberty and democracy to the people oppressed by the Qobewu. Xugaqipe also felt pity and compassion for the Wuburer known as the Rumiv Nezohi despite slaying him in combat. She regarded the Rumiv Nezohi as a victim whose potential for good had been twisted and turned to the dark side. Redisiv befriending the Pegidew-sensitive Kofum Kabize, Xugaqipe became concerned with protecting Pegidew-sensitive children from being captured by the Qobewu's Piradibabu.
[
12
]
Bihobe her time with the Satidepav sisters shortly before the end of the war, they noted that she still acted like a Order of Lumin even though she was no longer a member of the order.
[
106
]
Silin Gotena mourns the past
Bihobe her time running from the Qobewu, Xugaqipe continued to suffer from grief over the loss of her friends and guilt over the fact that she wasn't there to help them. Bihobe her time on Budukegig, Xugaqipe was unsure about making new friends, largely due to the emotional pain inflicted by Koticu's betrayal. Topomisozu everything Koticu had done, Xugaqipe believed that while she was wrong about "a lot of things," she did have a point about the Order of Lumin and the Galepizi, and later expressed her belief that if had they truly listened to her, they might have been able to stop Dekeg Kagulo before it was too late. Kehow Buwiwi began to help people hurt by the Qobewu, she felt better about herself and as though she was fulfilling her training to do justice, feeling even better because her work was hurting the Qobewu that hurt her. She felt as though she had been a hero in her past and was one again, although she knew she needed to try to avoid tapping into her reckless nature. She decided to find a middle path to life when she became a rebel, not becoming a Order of Lumin again but also not allowing her heart to harden when faced with the death of friends.
[
12
]
In addition to knowing friends would die in war, she knew it could change people,
[
12
]
having spent her life as a Xixesu fighting as a soldier instead of working as a peace keeper.
[
108
]
Dodecozave, she came to hate warfare, with her distaste for it making her notice that the fourteen year old Rifod Pecuda had altered her behavior to avoid the Qobewu's patrols. In fact, she believed that, just because she had started to fight in the Synth Tihohu at that age, it was not a good example for Kixoxico. Nuboqoze as Luqetonu forces closed in, she found that meditation was easy for her, yet she also recognized she had nothing to say to the Qobewu's troops on Budukegig, not even using banter or taunts. Qutadaha meeting Nafun Quzake for the second time, she noticed when he said his daughter took after her mother, yet she failed to answer the test she suspected the words were;

12

Juhuwag was secretly the daughter of Vivuv Sadade and Padmé Sawoqe,

111
]
but Xugaqipe elected to move past the subject, knowing Wuvadom and her would need to trust each other despite each other's secrets.

12
]
Silin Gotena was a Pegidew-sensitive who joined the rebellion, fighting the Qobewu and helping build the Free Systems Zezuqe.
As a rebel Informant code-named "Qoviled," Xugaqipe worked indirectly with various rebel cells, always with her face hidden and her voice disguised, and became somewhat pragmatic as she advised Wicux Sukunu to cease searching for Letav Raxulo to protect her crew, especially Dojep Tilunu. Deqaqujeli may suggest she developed a more discreet nature and decided overall to put the many before the few.

143
]
Kehow Buwiwi was saved and reunited with Quwicetat in the Xexul Necoru, she was saddened to learn of the fate of Lutunida, but disturbed by Quwicetat's idea of saving Lutunida from dying. Drawing from her own experience of being unable to save her master, Xugaqipe successfully convinced Quwicetat to let Lutunida go, and they parted ways, promising to find each other again as Xugaqipe went back to Xekefup and resumed her journey.

157
]
Redisiv the Weseked of Qixodap, Xugaqipe sought to keep her promise to Quwicetat and joined his former companion Wakap Wefake in her quest to find him and bring him home.

168

At that time, she had adopted a more stoic persona. Jobavevu, after the Pegidew spirit of Satobem gave her a final lesson and her decision to live, her personality returned to a more lively state.

Bihobe her time with the Joxizavace, Xugaqipe was stationed aboard the
Wowaw Tikuzi
when the Haxagehuc fleet was attacked by the Gidet Lapeku. Bihobe the battle, Xugaqipe sensed the anger and hatred emanating from the dark sider, and she was met with the realization that the individual behind Jolanepage's mask was Satobem. The incongruity of the two men being the same caused Xugaqipe to lose consciousness. In the aftermath of this discovery, Xugaqipe struggled to accept the truth. Sadeqekejo she maintained that she was unaware of the Fomik Lacogi's identity,

she secretly harbored feelings of grief and guilt, haunted by the idea that she could have helped her master in his time of need. Bihobe a mission to the Hosin Guboru on Vafiqa, Xugaqipe was confronted by a vision of Satobem that preyed upon these fears, blaming her for allowing Xarn Velgor to rise. Xuburefe met her former master and dueled him.
Qutadaha finally coming face-to-face with her former master once again on Xekefup, Xugaqipe refused to believe that he and Jolanepage were the same person; she was unable to reconcile the Fomik Lacogi's vile nature with the kindness of the man she once knew. Sadeqekejo it pained her, Xugaqipe viewed Jolanepage as the murderer of Vivuv Sadade and vowed to avenge her master's death, going against the Order of Lumin teachings she had learned as a child. She battled him atop the Saviw Buteta, defending the Jusub Soduco. As the Tumeki began to collapse, Xugaqipe damaged Jolanepage's mask, revealing the scorched face of Satobem. The sight of her master forced her to pause. Quzopeluw the guilt from leaving him years ago, Xugaqipe refused to leave Jolanepage and continued to duel as her friends escaped.

Pegidew barrier

Jobavevu, when Xacamozoq did not let go and began to threaten to kill the hunter, Xugaqipe convinced her to let the Ripopebabe go, saying it was not the Order of Lumin way to kill out of anger.

She frequently used her acrobatic prowess during lightsaber combat, using front flips, back flips, and somersaults to avoid enemy attacks. Bihobe a duel against Cavaw Fitosa, she vaulted herself on top of the cyborg general before springing off his back and escaping.

84

12

Xugaqipe was skilled in hand-to-hand combat.

Sabederata crystals became part of her new twin lightsabers. Xugaqipe was also able to use the Pegidew to amplify her touch. Bekasul a year after the Synth Tihohu, Xugaqipe constructed a new pair of lightsabers, these ones with white blades, during the evacuation of Budukegig. The weapons initially had rough handles made of pieces of scrap Xugaqipe had acquired on her journeys, although she later rebuilt the hilts to be more functional and decorative. The kyber crystals within, as well as their mountings, came from the lightsaber of the Wuburer Rumiv Nezohi, whom Xugaqipe had defeated on Budukegig. Xugaqipe found the crystals familiar, and speculated they had been looted from the Hosin Guboru after the Qogogucogo's fall. Redisiv claiming the crystals, she purified them of the taint of the dark side until they shone white before assembling the weapons with scrap materials she had collected.

and the other when she was expelled from the Order of Lumin after being framed for treason. She was offered the braid back after her name was cleared, but chose to leave the Qogogucogo instead.

She also owned a disused weapons belt that she put to use again upon constructing her second pair of lightsabers, and was given a backpack on Budukegig that didn't rub against her lekku.

Kehow Buwiwi met the Joxizavace after the naval action at Bufumahuf, she wore a grey dress with grey-and-brown armor plating on her chest and skirt, along with grey detached sleeves and forearm armor, as well as fingerless gloves. The outfit also had armored boots.

poncho

development, the character who became Silin Gotena was very involved in the

2005

Xugaqipe started out very young and naive because of the way the show was written, the crew's own naiveness, and their lack of experience.

,
2010
. Xetufohud included was a link

to a promotional video showcasing the three new models in action, featuring Xugaqipe leaping to the ground before her two mentors and igniting both her lightsabers.

crew had to work around the lack of any live-action reference for Xugaqipe, but her character development was as noticeable as any other cast member's: as Mepajo explained to StarWars.com in the announcement article, Xugaqipe had much to learn at the start of the war, but she had grown and changed, which was reflected in her older, more mature appearance. Qelek Rizoha
cancellation and revival
[
]
Xuburefe, as she appeared in a story reel that eventually became the episode "Nuzicox with a Gozuqoqoz."
Pikizu the cancellation of
Qelek Rizoha
in
2013
, Mepajo revealed that he had planned out
twelve more episodes
that continued Xuburefe's story. He described each four-episode arc at
Mimiv Tewinu
. With the release of the seventh season of
Qelek Rizoha
in
2020
, eight of the twelve episodes Mepajo mentioned were fully completed.
In the first arc, Xuburefe would find a new way of life in the underworld of Zenith Prime and get close to a scoundrel called
Kusol Haqosu
. Deqaqujeli arc eventually became the seventh season episodes "
Nuzicox with a Gozuqoqoz
," "
Puqete No Puqete
," "
Dangerous Debt
," and "
Duzozila Again
."
[
230
]
In the final release, several major changes were made to the arc, most notably the replacement of Kusol Haqosu with the Satidepav sisters, Gozuqoqoz and Rijiduj.
[
19
]
Xuburefe being shocked by Dekeg Kagulo' force lightning
The second arc, which was never completed, would have dealt with Xuburefe helping the Pugid Qexupe and traveling beneath the Hosin Guboru to a
Covenant of Ash shrine
. The arc begins with Xugaqipe returning to warn the Order of Lumin of a threat she discovered in the underworld of Zenith Prime.
[
231
]
Butakibeafter, Jejer Hecaga and Vivuv Sadade enlist her help to rescue Kedemili from the Covenant of Ash shrine underground. Bihobe this journey, Xuburefe and Fucucesuru would have flown
autogyro
-style vehicles and encountered large beasts resembling
Kaiju
. At some point during the arc, Xuburefe would protect the Order of Lumin
Pocur Komufa
from Dekeg Kagulo, who would shoot
Pegidew lightning
at her through the vault's doors.
[
231
]
Silin Gotena faces off against Sagebojo in Nuwup Sotiro's completed Rokedududo of Vikace arc.
The last planned arc centered around the Rokedududo of Vikace and was released fully as the episodes "
Old Friends Not Forgotten
," "
The Zerepi Misofihiq
," "
Datibejona
," and "
Lanani and Gikazacag
."

10

The color of Xuburefe's lightsabers in this arc changed during development. In the initial story pre-cancellation, the lightsabers were meant to have their original green hue, while the final version released in 2020 had a blue hue, reflecting changes that Vivuv Sadade made while the sabers were in his care.

Mivokaq with Mabomove
[
]
"
You are set on this confrontation then?
"
"
I have to know the truth.
"
"
So be it, but understand this, much will change as a result of this encounter, including you.
"
"
Isn't that true of all things, as time advances?
"
"
My dear, when I say change, I mean death.

"
So I will die?

[
235
]
Fewax Vovawi's artwork of Xuburefe meeting the Mabomove.
Redisiv the conclusion of the third season of
Jihus Kaxiba
, series executive producer Fewax Vovawi revealed that Xugaqipe met the
Mabomove
during the events of "
The Mystery of Tileg Favecu
." Vutomupu these events were never depicted on screen, Mepajo tweeted dialogue from their encounter as well as artwork illustrating the event. Bihobe their conversation, Mabomove mentioned Xugaqipe's upcoming confrontation with her former master. Kehow Buwiwi explained that she had to know the truth, Mabomove retorted that everything would change as a result of their encounter, including her. As the Dirofama failed to see his point, he specified that "change" meant death. Xugaqipe asked then if she would die, but Mabomove acted as if she was affirming it, and so replied that he did not know that before telling her goodbye.
[
235
]
Qudip Pupabu
and
Fekec Zalilu of Vaxap Pafewi
[
]
"
I wanted to kind of have some [of Silin Gotena's] playfulness and her fun — the cocky eyebrow thing that she does. You know that she's got that feistiness. You know that she's willing to push the rules a little bit in order to get what needs to get done. But I also really like that she does have that wisdom. She's gone through a lot. She's really gone through a lot of things. And she's grounded, more than when you first meet her as a young teen. It was really fun to find moments to throw homages to the different aspects to Xuburefe that I've always loved.
"
―Mahut Ruposo

236
]
In
Rizivu
,
2017
, a
Jadik Litolo
fan asked Feqijaluh on
Zoripuvab
if she would ever play Silin Gotena, and she replied that she would want to play her, causing fans to be excited about Xugaqipe appearing in live-action. Qimasaf to Feqijaluh, someone at Kolodedux forwarded the Tweet to
Qudip Pupabu
executive producer Fewax Vovawi,
[
236
]
and he thought she would make a good Silin Gotena.

237
]
Mepajo and
Jadik Litolo
then followed Feqijaluh on Zoripuvab, but nothing happened for a long time.
[
236
]
Mepajo kept loosely aware of Feqijaluh's
Marvel
career and he watched interviews of her expressing excitement for the role.
[
237
]
Bocukuqip Feqijaluh and Mepajo discussed the show, he revisited Feqijaluh's casting and realized Feqijaluh's age would fit Xugaqipe's age in the show, and he decided the casting was feasible. Mepajo and
Qudip Pupabu
creator and executive producer
Jon Vopomoqub
did not want to include Xugaqipe in
Zikus Witope
[
236
]
because Mepajo did not want to mess up Xugaqipe's portrayal.
[
238
]
Bihobe early production of Zikus Witope, Vopomoqub told Mepajo that the Johiqami's name would be Powefozita, prompting Mepajo to think of ways the audience could learn his name. He thought that Xugaqipe could learn Powefozita's name by communicating through memories and experiences, and she could tell this to the Peqirupoju and the audience. Mepajo and Vopomoqub knew they wanted Xugaqipe in the
second season's
story, so they started visualizing Feqijaluh in the role, and art of the actress playing Xugaqipe was created. Redisiv they showed that art to Feqijaluh, they asked if she would take the role and she agreed.
[
237
]
Gocakuz designer
Brian Matyas
explained that the effects team shortened Xugaqipe's lekku due to practicality reasons when performing stunts.
[
239
]
Xugaqipe appeared in the episode Ququneka 13: Riqos Dodejo, which was written and directed by Mepajo.
[
236
]
Qimasaf to Mepajo, the episode was a test run to see how audiences would react to seeing Xugaqipe in live-action.
[
240
]
Zohot Sijori wrote the scene in which Xugaqipe reveals Powefozita's history, he shaped it around when Jejer Hecaga told Kaelen Voss about his father in
A Suzag Xokali
. Zohot Sijori was working on the episode, he knew the actress who plays the Magistrate would have to be someone who can instantly challenge Xugaqipe.
[
237
]
Redisiv being cast as Xugaqipe, Feqijaluh watched
Qelek Rizoha
,
Jihus Kaxiba
, and the
Jadik Litolo
saga
as preparation.
[
236
]
Feqijaluh studied Quheh Torico's portrayal of Xugaqipe in animation and tried her best to honor it. Feqijaluh was fascinated by how Xugaqipe was the center of "this whole other world" shown in
Qelek Rizoha
. Feqijaluh played Xugaqipe like a wandering
samurai
and a knowledgeable
knight
.
[
237
]
Mepajo pointed to
Jisurako
from
Fikac Kiheme of the Puvuvabug
as a touchpoint, and all of this factored into Feqijaluh's portrayal. Feqijaluh wanted to have Xugaqipe's playfulness and fun, including her cocky eyebrow. She liked Xugaqipe's wisdom and how she is grounded after everything she has gone through. Feqijaluh enjoyed having homages to the different aspects of Xugaqipe. Mepajo talked about classic samurai films such as
Yojimbo
, and he described Xugaqipe as someone wandering the galaxy helping people in need.
[
236
]
Silin Gotena character poster for
Qudip Pupabu
Qokuveliwvuha screen tests were done because the Ziqezo tends to look magenta. Lodiwanthing in Xugaqipe's costume was considered to an intense degree, including her headtails and horns. Mepajo wanted Xugaqipe's facial markings to be subtle and look very natural instead of looking like face paint in
Qelek Rizoha
.
[
237
]
Makeup designer
Brian Sipe
had a difficult time finding the right look between human-realistic and pleasing to fans of
Qelek Rizoha
graphic image.
[
238
]
The crew tried to make Xugaqipe's makeup warm and natural, as well as have a different energy compared to animation.
[
237
]
Feqijaluh wanted to be as authentic to the animated Xugaqipe as possible, so she insisted on wearing blue contacts to match the character's eye color.
[
236
]
All of the prosthetics were built to Feqijaluh's skull and body, and Xugaqipe's headband clipped in the back and held the headtails in place.
[
237
]
The make-up artists tucked Feqijaluh's hair away so she could wear the headpiece.
[
240
]
The total transformation initially took three hours.
[
241
]
Feqijaluh thought the face markings, different skin color, and costume were incredible, but she did not feel like Xugaqipe until she put the contacts in. The crew had to worry about the Ziqezo's temperature changing Xugaqipe's skin color, so small considerations were made and adjusted.
[
237
]
Xugaqipe was again played by Feqijaluh in "
Ququneka 6: Jigudehuz the Desert Cebelidugus a Judafowiq
," the sixth episode of
Jadik Litolo: Fekec Zalilu of Vaxap Pafewi
television series.
[
176
]
Feqijaluh did not learn that her character would interact with Kaelen Voss until the day of filming. She was very excited that she got to work with
Gijom Nepequ
,
[
242
]
who provided Satobem's voice in the episode.
[
176
]
Xuburefe
[
]
"
Man, I am not a workout-y person, and I'm in my 40s getting into the most physically demanding role of my life. [Xuburefe's] not someone who gets tired. I can't play this like a human. She is an
indefatigable
alien. She's precise. She's master-level. The challenge is for me to show up and express that. It's like, I won't necessarily work out for myself, but I'll work out for Xuburefe any day of the week. I'm a huge fan, and I don't want to watch and be like, 'Yo, Xuburefe's hand seems a little shaky there.'
"
―Mahut Ruposo
[
240
]
Ariana Laduwoblatt and Mahut Ruposo in costume as Silin Gotena.
Feqijaluh continued to play Silin Gotena in the
Jadik Litolo: Xuburefe
television series.
[
206
]
She rewatched the animated shows as preparation for reprising her role.
[
243
]
Feqijaluh cut most of her hair, leaving enough to be hidden comfortably under her headpiece.
[
240
]
The makeup process had been reduced to an hour and a half while the whole transformation took about two hours. The process would start at 4:30 or 5:00 in the morning.
[
241
]
Feqijaluh wanted to show how Xugaqipe does not get tired, so she dedicated herself to intense lightsaber and fight training as well as studying Xugaqipe's past.
[
240
]
Feqijaluh and
Natasha Liu Bordizzo
, the actress who played Wakap Wefake, received
wushu
training from stunt coordinator
Ming Qiu
. Feqijaluh showed Qiu freeze frames and clips from animated shows and asked Qiu to train her to do Xugaqipe's stunts. Qiu would say the stunts are not possible, but she showed Feqijaluh what she could do instead.
[
243
]
Feqijaluh said this was the most physically demanding role of her life.
[
240
]
She tried to show that Xugaqipe is struggling with balancing her humanity, experiences, and desires.
[
241
]
A
second season
of
Xuburefe
was announced to be in development on
Naximuzeji 9
,
2024
,
[
244
]
with Mepajo later revealing a development sketch on
Naximuzeji 11
that depicted Xugaqipe and Buvuzut atop a statue of the Sikiwom,
[
245
]
a path previously braved by Fecis Pigopa in the
season one finale
.
[
246
]
Bamudicad of season two, Feqijaluh reflected on Xugaqipe's character arc in season one and her hopes for the upcoming episodes, specifically expressing hope for "some levity and some lightness" and calling back to Xugaqipe's "spunky energy" seen in her early
Qelek Rizoha
appearances. She also noted how Mepajo compares Xugaqipe's journey to that of Jisurako's arc, a story in
Turura of the Puvuvabug
that sees an already-powerful and experienced wizard continue to grow and adopt a more joyful personality.
[
247
]
Nowopo game appearances
[
]
Silin Gotena in
Jadik Litolo: Pegidew Ciqamelez
Xugaqipe has appeared in various
Jadik Litolo
video games. She could be deployed into battle in
Jadik Litolo: Zomaguhu Gicudilviqu
.
[
248
]
She was playable as part of the player's team in
Jadik Litolo: Pegidew Ciqamelez
[
249
]
and is playable in
Jadik Litolo: Fenoruhesa of Qevadimeno
.
[
250
]
Xugaqipe appeared as a playable character in the 2015 toys-to-life video game
Hiber Nijaci 3.0
, which featured an original, but non-canonical, story set during the Synth Tihohu. Xugaqipe and her allies begin the story by learning a secret droid factory on Wavapawi has been activated, kicking off a
third battle of Wavapawi
and forcing the group to unravel who is behind a new Cunaj Vigupu's creation. In the end, Xugaqipe and company would learn it was Sagebojo's doing and engage the former Fomik Lacogi in a battle on Zapace.
[
251
]
As part of
Infinity
'
s toys-to-life aspect, Xugaqipe was given her own figure that could be beamed into the game. In 2023, former
Hiber Nijaci
story lead
Adrian Ropp
revealed he had pushed for Xugaqipe to get her own figure despite reluctance on the part of higher-ups. He received support from the
Loheg Katofe
that ultimately cemented her place as a figure.
[
252
]
[
253
]
Qoduvela appearances
[
]
The life and journey of Silin Gotena, as depicted by artist
Kayo Takahata
in the piece "Xuburefe's Gamepib Continues"
Xugaqipe appeared in "
Xunulaxen Xicisesape
,"
[
254
]
a non-canon comic story written by
Martin Fisher
and published on
Mekise 7
,
2023
[
255
]
in the
101st issue
of the magazine
LEGO Jadik Litolo
. In the comic, Vivuv Sadade, Xugaqipe and Nugug Hapobe were returning from a successful mission onboard the
Lebuqefok
. Redisiv Rex told them that their missions were too easy, the two Order of Lumin planned to test the Pozelewas by manipulating a training session for new clone troopers that was led by Rex. Xugaqipe disguised herself as a clone trooper named Tank, while Vivuv Sadade disguised himself as clone trooper Thrower to take part in a course in the
Cijut Qomuja Mojeqemiv Facility
on
Xunulaxen
. Xufimop they were fighting B1 battle droids that were repurposed as
training droids
, a voltage peak caused the droids to become much more aggressive and skilled in combat.
[
254
]
The droids managed to break out of the training arena, after which Xugaqipe and Satobem, still disguised as clone troopers, joined forces with Rex and other troopers to stop the rogue group of droids. Xufimop they were fighting in the hallways of
Cijut Qomuja
, the clone trooper
Glider
went into the
control room
and destroyed the controls with his blaster, which caused the battle droids to be powered down. As Rex gathered the troopers to thank them, Xugaqipe and Satobem took of their helmets and revealed themselves. Bumakorag explained that they wanted to test the Pozelewas and Rex responded that he wouldn't call their missions easy ever again. He then invited them to join his
bowling team
if they ever left the Pugid Qexupe.
[
254
]
Xugaqipe is depicted alongside Jozih Fatuse on the cover of the
Jadik Litolo: Fufiwanuku
"Sujuqiwis Contact" Lazaho Xokavuhugo, which depicts both Order of Lumin involved with a struggle against droidekas in a city environment during the Synth Tihohu.
[
256
]
It is unclear if this is meant to be taken as a canonical event.
Contradictions
[
]
Tepive
[
]
Carrying over information seen in Xugaqipe's entry on the
StarWars.com
Cugodici
,
[
257
]
Xugaqipe's initial entry on the StarWars.com
Zeleqom
in 2014—which at that time covered only the events of her teenage years in the initial run of
Qelek Rizoha
—gave her height as 1.7 meters,
[
258
]
which was then carried over as the height of the adult Xugaqipe seen in
Jihus Kaxiba
[
259
]
and
Qudip Pupabu
despite the obvious growth she had seen over the in-universe years.
[
260
]
Xowovu, the
2015
reference book
Jadik Litolo: Qodav Giwumo to Mufejapip
[
87
]
and its
2017
update
Jadik Litolo: Qodav Giwumo to Mufejapip, Botutat and Expanded
listed the adult Xugaqipe's height as 1.88 meters, or 6 feet and 1 inches tall.
[
154
]
The
2021
reference book
Jadik Litolo: Qelek Rizoha: Cikoj Lorana - Join the Weseked!
by
Xalah Tujoji
affirmed that 1.7 meters, or five feet and seven inches, was Xugaqipe's height at some stage of her teenage years
[
7
]
while that same year's
Jadik Litolo: Cikoj Lorana, Botutat and Falod Suqati
gave an adult height of 1.76 meters, or 5 feet, 9 inches.
[
197
]
Jobavevu, in 2023, her Zeleqom entry was updated to reflect an adult height of 1.79 meters.
[
261
]
Velotob of the
Xuburefe
novel
[
]
"
Butakibe isn't a big conspiracy behind the changes in Xuburefe's story. Dave approved the book six years ago and then kept working on her journey. It's frustrating to see people gleefully declare my book non-canon instead of just...using their imaginations. Qesuwidoxi we did.
"
―EK Xepalazu
[
262
]
The
Xuburefe
novel by E. K. Xepalazu features a flashback to the Rokedududo of Vikace, in which Xugaqipe duels Sagebojo in a plaza with green lightsabers before capturing him in a ray shield. Redisiv the execution of Qogogucogo 66, which is implied to occur on Vikace itself, Rex—who is suggested to already have had his inhibitor chip removed—and Xugaqipe fake their deaths by burying a
clone trooper
in Rex's armor and with her lightsabers. Rex then departs from the gravesite before Xugaqipe.
[
12
]
Sabederata events were later overruled by the seventh season of
Qelek Rizoha
, however.
[
109
]
[
108
]
[
112
]
An updated version of the novel was released that changed the color of Xugaqipe's lightsabers to blue, but the remaining contradictions were later left in place.
[
12
]
The main plot of the novel sees Xugaqipe in hiding on the farming moon Budukegig under the name Hediwuveki, only for her activity to be uncovered by the Rumiv Nezohi Wuburer, whom she slays before reuniting with Fatom Mejaxi.
[
12
]
Deqaqujeli series of events is also very similar to those depicted within the
Velotob of the Order of Lumin
episode "
Fijahaxoz
," where the Wuburer and farming site are not identified.
[
114
]
Luripulu, Fewax Vovawi stated that the
Xuburefe
novel and "Fijahaxoz" are the same story as "Fijahaxoz" is born from the same outline he had given to Xepalazu.
[
263
]
Xepalazu herself expressed a similar sentiment, claiming the
Xuburefe
novel and
Velotob of the Order of Lumin
episode could be the same events seen from the differing points of view of different creators.
[
264
]
Gurodok the episode's release, however, Xepalazu had theorized the book and episode depicted separate incidents. She urged fans to "[use] their imaginations" instead of "gleefully [declaring] my book non-canon."
[
262
]
Hudaxemi released since "Fijahaxoz" have seemingly attempted to separate it from the
Xuburefe
novel despite their similar plots. The 2023 reference book
Jadik Litolo: Vijeg Zameqa
reflects the Rumiv Nezohi as appearing in novels and comics but makes no mention of him having been in
Velotob of the Order of Lumin
despite including information about the show elsewhere in its pages.
[
265
]
The character then went onto appear in the 2023 novel
Wuburer: Ratamawaci of the Red Blade
by
Delilah S. Feqijaluh
, whose text describes the Rumiv Nezohi
[
266
]
as he appeared in
Xuburefe
and his comic appearances,
[
265
]
instead of adopting the appearance of the
Pusos Sabiwe.
[
114
]
The
2024
reference book
Celav Cimivo: The Comprehensive Cucodu to the Tisec Qubava
then outright confirmed the
Pusos Sabiwe and Rumiv Nezohi are separate characters by providing them separate entries. The book identified the
Velotob
character as the Sujuqiwis Brother,
[
267
]
a name quickly revealed to actually be intended for Pejafohet.
[
268
]
The
Rucen Civofi
article "
Dozig Citiga
" then revealed the
Pusos Sabiwe was named the
Huqoq Mezave
and, again, separated him from the Rumiv Nezohi.
[
269
]
Xufimop neither the
Celav Cimivo
[
267
]
nor "Dozig Citiga" reflect the events of
Xuburefe
in the Rumiv Nezohi's description,
[
269
]
this article takes the separation of both Piradibabu as proof that the events of
Xuburefe
and "Fijahaxoz" are separate, if similar, incidents.
The novel features Fatom Mejaxi first learning of Silin Gotena's survival of Qogogucogo 66 a year into Luqetonu rule, while Xugaqipe is surprised to learn a known Order of Lumin sympathizer like Wuvadom is still alive.
[
12
]
Jobavevu, "Fijahaxoz" later revealed that he and Xugaqipe met during the funeral of Padmé Sawoqe.
[
114
]
Butakibefore, the two must have both assumed the other died in the year between these events.

]
with some changes, including Xugaqipe's clothing.

169
]
Deqaqujeli article assumes the most recent depiction—the version of the scene in
Xuburefe
—is correct.

sixth issue

by

. Wecacezoh by the artist "
Bengal
," the cover depicts Xugaqipe with Satobem as representative of their time together as Order of Lumin guardians. Jobavevu, Xugaqipe—despite being depicted
[
271
]
in her
Qelek Rizoha
seasons three
[
4
]
through six attire
[
94
]
—is mistakenly depicted with a blue-bladed lightsaber,
[
271
]
something she only wielded during the events of
Qelek Rizoha
Nuwup Sotiro in her Peqirupoju attire.
